    PROCEDURE PostTransportReceipt@8053002(TransportLine_P@8053000 : Record 39;QtyToBeReceied_P@8053008 : Decimal;QtyToBeReceiedBase_P@8053009 : Integer;OCNo_P@8053011 : Code[20]);
    VAR
      PurchOrderHeader_L@8053001 : Record 38;
      PurchRcptHeader_L@8053002 : Record 120;
      PurchRcptLine_L@8053003 : Record 121;
      TempDocDim2@8053004 : TEMPORARY Record 357;
      TempJnlLineDim@8053005 : TEMPORARY Record 356;
      TempHandlingSpecification2@8053007 : TEMPORARY Record 336;
      ItemEntryRelation@8053006 : Record 6507;
    BEGIN
      // --> 20131023-Log13-CMC02.02.00-WSA01
      PurchOrderHeader_L.GET(TransportLine_P."Document Type",TransportLine_P."Document No.");
      PurchRcptHeader_L.INIT;
      PurchRcptHeader_L.TRANSFERFIELDS(PurchOrderHeader_L);
      PurchRcptHeader_L."No." := OCNo_P;
      PurchRcptHeader_L."Order No." := PurchOrderHeader_L."No.";
      PurchRcptHeader_L."Posting Date" := WORKDATE;
      PurchRcptHeader_L."Document Date" := PurchOrderHeader_L."Document Date";
      PurchRcptHeader_L."No. Printed" := 0;
      PurchRcptHeader_L.INSERT;
      PurchSetup.GET;
      IF PurchSetup."Copy Comments Order to Receipt" THEN BEGIN
        CopyPurchCommentLines(
          PurchOrderHeader_L."Document Type",PurchCommentLine."Document Type"::Receipt,
          PurchOrderHeader_L."No.",PurchRcptHeader."No.");
        PurchRcptHeader.COPYLINKS(PurchOrderHeader_L);
      END;

      PurchRcptLine_L.INIT;
      PurchRcptLine_L.TRANSFERFIELDS(TransportLine_P);
      PurchRcptLine_L."Posting Date" := PurchRcptHeader."Posting Date";
      PurchRcptLine_L."Document No." := PurchRcptHeader_L."No.";
      PurchRcptLine_L.Quantity := QtyToBeReceied_P;
      PurchRcptLine_L."Quantity (Base)" := QtyToBeReceiedBase_P;
      PurchRcptLine_L."Quantity Invoiced" := 0;
      PurchRcptLine_L."Qty. Invoiced (Base)" := 0;
      PurchRcptLine_L."Order No." := TransportLine_P."Document No.";
      PurchRcptLine_L."Order Line No." := TransportLine_P."Line No.";
      PurchRcptLine_L."Qty. Rcd. Not Invoiced" :=
        PurchRcptLine_L.Quantity - PurchRcptLine_L."Quantity Invoiced";

      ItemJnlLine.INIT;
      ItemJnlLine."Source Posting Group" := PurchOrderHeader_L."Vendor Posting Group";
      ItemJnlLine."Salespers./Purch. Code" := PurchOrderHeader_L."Purchaser Code";
      ItemJnlLine."Country/Region Code" := PurchOrderHeader_L."VAT Country/Region Code";
      ItemJnlLine."Reason Code" := PurchOrderHeader_L."Reason Code";
      ItemJnlLine."Posting No. Series" := PurchOrderHeader_L."Posting No. Series";
      ItemJnlLine."Item No." := TransportLine_P."No.";
      ItemJnlLine.Description := TransportLine_P.Description;
      ItemJnlLine."Shortcut Dimension 1 Code" := TransportLine_P."Shortcut Dimension 1 Code";
      ItemJnlLine."Shortcut Dimension 2 Code" := TransportLine_P."Shortcut Dimension 2 Code";
      ItemJnlLine."Location Code" := TransportLine_P."Location Code";
      ItemJnlLine."Inventory Posting Group" := TransportLine_P."Posting Group";
      ItemJnlLine."Gen. Bus. Posting Group" := TransportLine_P."Gen. Bus. Posting Group";
      ItemJnlLine."Gen. Prod. Posting Group" := TransportLine_P."Gen. Prod. Posting Group";
      ItemJnlLine."Applies-to Entry" := TransportLine_P."Appl.-to Item Entry";
      ItemJnlLine."Transaction Type" := TransportLine_P."Transaction Type";
      ItemJnlLine."Transport Method" := TransportLine_P."Transport Method";
      ItemJnlLine."Entry/Exit Point" := TransportLine_P."Entry Point";
      ItemJnlLine.Area := TransportLine_P.Area;
      ItemJnlLine."Transaction Specification" := TransportLine_P."Transaction Specification";
      ItemJnlLine."Drop Shipment" := TransportLine_P."Drop Shipment";
      ItemJnlLine."Posting Date" := WORKDATE;
      ItemJnlLine."Document Date" := PurchOrderHeader_L."Document Date";
      ItemJnlLine."Entry Type" := ItemJnlLine."Entry Type"::Purchase;
      ItemJnlLine."Document No." := PurchRcptHeader_L."No.";
      ItemJnlLine."External Document No." := PurchOrderHeader_L."No.";
      ItemJnlLine."Document Type" := ItemJnlLine."Document Type"::"Purchase Receipt";
      ItemJnlLine."Document Line No." := TransportLine_P."Line No.";
      ItemJnlLine.Quantity := QtyToBeReceied_P;
      ItemJnlLine."Quantity (Base)" := QtyToBeReceiedBase_P;
      ItemJnlLine."Invoiced Quantity" := 0;
      ItemJnlLine."Invoiced Qty. (Base)" := 0;
      ItemJnlLine."Unit Cost" := TransportLine_P."Unit Cost (LCY)";
      ItemJnlLine."Source Currency Code" := PurchOrderHeader_L."Currency Code";
      ItemJnlLine."Unit Cost (ACY)" := TransportLine_P."Unit Cost";
      ItemJnlLine."Source Type" := ItemJnlLine."Source Type"::Vendor;
      ItemJnlLine."Source No." := TransportLine_P."Buy-from Vendor No.";
      ItemJnlLine."Invoice-to Source No." := TransportLine_P."Pay-to Vendor No.";
      ItemJnlLine."Source Code" := SrcCode;
      ItemJnlLine."Variant Code" := TransportLine_P."Variant Code";
      ItemJnlLine."Item Category Code" := TransportLine_P."Item Category Code";
      ItemJnlLine."Product Group Code" := TransportLine_P."Product Group Code";
      ItemJnlLine."Bin Code" := TransportLine_P."Bin Code";
      ItemJnlLine."Purchasing Code" := TransportLine_P."Purchasing Code";
      ItemJnlLine."Prod. Order No." := TransportLine_P."Prod. Order No.";
      ItemJnlLine."Unit of Measure Code" := TransportLine_P."Unit of Measure Code";
      ItemJnlLine."Qty. per Unit of Measure" := TransportLine_P."Qty. per Unit of Measure";
      ItemJnlLine."Applies-to Entry" := 0;
      ItemJnlLine."Shipment Method Code" := PurchOrderHeader_L."Shipment Method Code";
      NewFieldsManagement_G.ItemLedgerEntry_PurchLine(ItemJnlLine,TransportLine_P);
      TempJnlLineDim.DELETEALL;

      G_CMCDimension.DimFromPurch(TempDocDim2,TransportLine_P);
      DimMgt.CopyDocDimToJnlLineDim(TempDocDim2,TempJnlLineDim);
      ItemJnlPostLine.RunWithCheck(ItemJnlLine,TempJnlLineDim);
      // Handle Item Tracking
      IF ItemJnlPostLine.CollectTrackingSpecification(TempHandlingSpecification2) THEN BEGIN
        IF TempHandlingSpecification2.FINDSET THEN
          REPEAT
            TempTrackingSpecification := TempHandlingSpecification2;
            TempTrackingSpecification."Source Type" := DATABASE::"Purchase Line";
            TempTrackingSpecification."Source Subtype" := TransportLine_P."Document Type";
            TempTrackingSpecification."Source ID" := TransportLine_P."Document No.";
            TempTrackingSpecification."Source Batch Name" := '';
            TempTrackingSpecification."Source Prod. Order Line" := 0;
            TempTrackingSpecification."Source Ref. No." := TransportLine_P."Line No.";
            IF TempTrackingSpecification.INSERT THEN;
            ItemEntryRelation.INIT;
            ItemEntryRelation."Item Entry No." := TempHandlingSpecification2."Entry No.";
            ItemEntryRelation."Serial No." := TempHandlingSpecification2."Serial No.";
            ItemEntryRelation."Lot No." := TempHandlingSpecification2."Lot No.";
            ItemEntryRelation."Source Type" := DATABASE::"Purch. Rcpt. Line";
            ItemEntryRelation."Source ID" := PurchOrderHeader_L."Receiving No.";
            ItemEntryRelation."Source Ref. No." := TransportLine_P."Line No.";
            ItemEntryRelation."Order No." := TransportLine_P."Document No.";
            ItemEntryRelation."Order Line No." := TransportLine_P."Line No.";
            ItemEntryRelation.INSERT;
          UNTIL TempHandlingSpecification2.NEXT = 0;
      END;

      IF PurchRcptLine_L.Quantity <> 0 THEN BEGIN
        PurchRcptLine_L."Item Rcpt. Entry No." := ItemJnlLine."Item Shpt. Entry No.";
        PurchRcptLine_L."Item Charge Base Amount" := TransportLine_P."Line Amount"
      END;
      PurchRcptLine_L.INSERT;

      TransportLine_P."Quantity Received" += QtyToBeReceied_P;
      TransportLine_P."Qty. Received (Base)" += QtyToBeReceiedBase_P;
      TransportLine_P."Qty. to Receive" := QtyToBeReceied_P;
      TransportLine_P."Qty. to Receive (Base)" := QtyToBeReceiedBase_P;
      UpdateBlanketOrderLine(TransportLine_P,TRUE,FALSE,FALSE);
      TransportLine_P.InitOutstanding;
      TransportLine_P.InitQtyToReceive;
      TransportLine_P.MODIFY;
      // <-- 20131023-Log13-CMC02.02.00-WSA01
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE CopyPurchCommentLines@148(FromDocumentType@1000 : Integer;ToDocumentType@1001 : Integer;FromNumber@1002 : Code[20];ToNumber@1003 : Code[20]);
    VAR
      PurchCommentLine@1004 : Record 43;
      PurchCommentLine2@1005 : Record 43;
    BEGIN
      PurchCommentLine.SETRANGE("Document Type",FromDocumentType);
      PurchCommentLine.SETRANGE("No.",FromNumber);
      IF PurchCommentLine.FINDSET THEN
        REPEAT
         PurchCommentLine2 := PurchCommentLine;
         PurchCommentLine2."Document Type" := ToDocumentType;
         PurchCommentLine2."No." := ToNumber;
         PurchCommentLine2.INSERT;
       UNTIL PurchCommentLine.NEXT = 0;
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE CheckCellMandatory@8053006(VAR PurchLine_P@1000 : Record 39;TypeMvt_P@8053002 : 'Receive,Ship');
    VAR
      PurchLine2_L@1001 : Record 39;
      WhseValidateSourceLine@1003 : Codeunit 5777;
      ShowError@1002 : Boolean;
      "<<3LI LOCALS>>"@8060982 : Integer;
      BaselineMgt_L@8060983 : Codeunit 8060982;
      CduItemJnlPostLine_L@8053000 : Codeunit 22;
      recItem_L@8053001 : Record 27;
    BEGIN
      //------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // CheckCellMandatory
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Input:
      //   - PurchLine_P
      //   - TypeMvt_P
      // Output:
      //------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // --> 20140105-WI00-CMC02.02.00-1386-SD01
      PurchLine2_L.COPY(PurchLine_P);
      PurchLine2_L.SETRANGE(Type,PurchLine2_L.Type::Item);
      PurchLine2_L.SETRANGE("Drop Shipment",FALSE);

      CASE TypeMvt_P OF
        TypeMvt_P::Receive:PurchLine2_L.SETFILTER("Qty. to Receive",'<>0');
        TypeMvt_P::Ship:PurchLine2_L.SETFILTER("Return Qty. to Ship",'<>0');
      END;

      IF PurchLine2_L.FINDSET THEN
        REPEAT
          GetLocation(PurchLine2_L."Location Code");
          recItem_L.GET(PurchLine2_L."No.");
          IF CduItemJnlPostLine_L.ItemToReverse(recItem_L,Location."Cell Item Filter View") THEN BEGIN
            PurchLine2_L.TESTFIELD("Cell Code")
          END ELSE
            PurchLine2_L.TESTFIELD("Cell Code",'');
        UNTIL PurchLine2_L.NEXT = 0;
      // <-- 20140105-WI00-CMC02.02.00-1386-SD01
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateGLDescription@8053007(Rec_P@8053000 : Record 38;VAR Description_P@8053001 : Text[50]);
    VAR
      Contact_L@8053002 : Record 5050;
      CMCActivity_L@8053003 : Record 8052985;
      CMCFamily_L@8053004 : Record 8052986;
      CMCSubFamily_L@8053005 : Record 8052987;
      CMCSpecies_L@8053006 : Record 8052988;
      PurchaseLine_L@8053007 : Record 39;
      Item_L@8053009 : Record 27;
      ActivityTXT_L@8053008 : Text[50];
      MentionTXT_L@8053010 : Text[50];
      ApportTxt0_L@8053011 : TextConst 'ENU=Apport;FRA=Apport';
      ApportTxt1_L@8053012 : TextConst 'ENU=Collection;FRA=Collecte';
      ApportTxt2_L@8053013 : TextConst 'ENU=Complement;FRA=Compl'ment';
      ApportTxt3_L@8053014 : TextConst 'ENU=Vat Regul.;FRA=R'gul TVA';
      ApportTxt4_L@8053015 : TextConst 'ENU=Av. Harvest;FRA=Moy. Moisson';
      RecCollectionContract_L@1000000000 : Record 8052983;
    BEGIN
      // --> 20140606-FINA27/001-CMC02.02.00-AKH01
      WITH Rec_P DO BEGIN
        // --> 20150227-FINA27/001-CMC02.02.00-2793-BRA01
        //BEFORE
        {
        IF NOT ("Document Type" IN ["Document Type"::Invoice,"Document Type"::"Credit Memo"]) THEN
          EXIT;
        }
        // <-- 20150227-FINA27/001-CMC02.02.00-2793-BRA01

        Contact_L.RESET;
        Contact_L.SETRANGE(Type,Contact_L.Type::Company);
        Contact_L.SETRANGE("Vendor No.","Buy-from Vendor No.");
        IF NOT Contact_L.FINDFIRST THEN
          EXIT;
        PurchSetup.GET;
        // --> 20160219-Wi00-ADV01.01.00-2713-JCD01
        {
        //BEFORE
        IF Contact_L."Contact Type" <> Contact_L."Contact Type"::Farm THEN BEGIN
        //AFTER
        }
        IF (Contact_L."Contact Type" <> Contact_L."Contact Type"::Farm) OR (Contact_L."Intra Group Code" <> '') THEN BEGIN
        // <-- 20160219-Wi00-ADV01.01.00-2713-JCD01
          IF PurchSetup."Exclude Farmer G/L Label" = '' THEN
            EXIT;
          IF "Document Type"="Document Type"::Invoice THEN
            Description_P := COPYSTR(STRSUBSTNO(PurchSetup."Exclude Farmer G/L Label",FORMAT("Document Type"),
              "Vendor Invoice No.","Document Date"),1,50);
          IF "Document Type"="Document Type"::"Credit Memo" THEN
            Description_P := COPYSTR(STRSUBSTNO(PurchSetup."Exclude Farmer G/L Label",FORMAT("Document Type"),
              "Vendor Cr. Memo No.","Document Date"),1,50);
        END ELSE BEGIN
          IF PurchSetup."Farmer G/L Label" = '' THEN
            EXIT;
          // --> 20160726-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3152-NED
          IF "Collection Invoice Type" <> "Collection Invoice Type" :: "Collection Prepay Invoice" THEN BEGIN
          // <-- 20160726-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3152-NED
            PurchaseLine_L.RESET;
            PurchaseLine_L.SETCURRENTKEY("Document Type","Document No.",Type);
            PurchaseLine_L.SETRANGE("Document Type","Document Type");
            PurchaseLine_L.SETRANGE("Document No.","No.");
            PurchaseLine_L.SETRANGE(Type,PurchaseLine_L.Type::Item);
            IF NOT PurchaseLine_L.FINDFIRST THEN
              EXIT;
          // --> 20160726-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3152-NED
          END;
          // <-- 20160726-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3152-NED
          CASE "Collection Invoice Type" OF
            "Collection Invoice Type"::" ":
              MentionTXT_L := ApportTxt0_L;
            // --> 20160726-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3152-NED
            //"Collection Invoice Type"::Collection:
            "Collection Invoice Type" :: Collection,"Collection Invoice Type" :: "Collection Prepay Invoice" : BEGIN
            // <-- 20160726-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3152-NED
              MentionTXT_L := ApportTxt1_L;
            END;
            "Collection Invoice Type"::Supplement:
              MentionTXT_L := ApportTxt2_L;
            "Collection Invoice Type"::"VAT Regulation":
              MentionTXT_L := ApportTxt3_L;
            "Collection Invoice Type"::"Average Harvest":
              MentionTXT_L := ApportTxt4_L;
            // --> 20160609-WI00-ADV01.01.00-2914-JCD01
            "Collection Invoice Type"::"Supplement Commitment":
              MentionTXT_L := Text60002;
            // <-- 20160609-WI00-ADV01.01.00-2914-JCD01
            ELSE
              MentionTXT_L := '';
          END;
        // --> 20160629-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3037-SDE
        IF "Collection Invoice Type" <> "Collection Invoice Type"::"Average Harvest" THEN
        BEGIN
        // <-- 20160629-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3037-SDE
          // --> 20160726-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3152-NED
          IF "Collection Invoice Type" <> "Collection Invoice Type" :: "Collection Prepay Invoice" THEN BEGIN
          // <-- 20160726-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3152-NED
          Item_L.GET(PurchaseLine_L."No.");
          CASE  TRUE OF
            (CMCSpecies_L.GET(PurchaseLine_L."Species Code") AND (CMCSpecies_L."Purchase G/L Label" <> '')) :
              ActivityTXT_L := CMCSpecies_L."Purchase G/L Label";
            (CMCSubFamily_L.GET(PurchaseLine_L."Sub-Family Code") AND (CMCSubFamily_L."Purchase G/L Label" <> '')):
              ActivityTXT_L := CMCSubFamily_L."Purchase G/L Label";
            (CMCFamily_L.GET(PurchaseLine_L."Family Code") AND (CMCFamily_L."Purchase G/L Label" <> '')):
              ActivityTXT_L := CMCFamily_L."Purchase G/L Label";
            (CMCActivity_L.GET(PurchaseLine_L."Activity Code") AND (CMCActivity_L."Purchase G/L Label" <> '')):
              ActivityTXT_L := CMCActivity_L."Purchase G/L Label";
          END;
          // --> 20160726-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3152-NED
          END ELSE BEGIN
            RecCollectionContract_L.GET("Contract No.");
            Item_L.GET(RecCollectionContract_L."Item No.");
            CASE  TRUE OF
              (CMCSpecies_L.GET(Item_L."Species Code") AND (CMCSpecies_L."Purchase G/L Label" <> '')) :
                ActivityTXT_L := CMCSpecies_L."Purchase G/L Label";
              (CMCSubFamily_L.GET(Item_L."Sub-Family Code") AND (CMCSubFamily_L."Purchase G/L Label" <> '')):
                ActivityTXT_L := CMCSubFamily_L."Purchase G/L Label";
              (CMCFamily_L.GET(Item_L."Family Code") AND (CMCFamily_L."Purchase G/L Label" <> '')):
                ActivityTXT_L := CMCFamily_L."Purchase G/L Label";
              (CMCActivity_L.GET(Item_L."Activity Code") AND (CMCActivity_L."Purchase G/L Label" <> '')):
                ActivityTXT_L := CMCActivity_L."Purchase G/L Label";
            END;
          // <-- 20160726-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3152-NED
          END;
        // --> 20160629-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3037-SDE
        END;
        // <-- 20160629-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3037-SDE
          // --> 20160609-WI00-ADV01.01.00-2914-JCD01
          IF "Collection Invoice Type" = "Collection Invoice Type"::"Supplement Commitment" THEN
            ActivityTXT_L := '';
          // <-- 20160609-WI00-ADV01.01.00-2914-JCD01

          // --> 20150227-FINA27/001-CMC02.02.00-2793-BRA01
          //BEFORE
          //Description_P := COPYSTR(STRSUBSTNO(PurchSetup."Farmer G/L Label",FORMAT("Document Type"),MentionTXT_L,
          //AFTER
          Description_P := COPYSTR(STRSUBSTNO(PurchSetup."Farmer G/L Label",FORMAT(GenJnlLine."Document Type"),MentionTXT_L,
          // <-- 20150227-FINA27/001-CMC02.02.00-2793-BRA01
              GenJnlLineDocNo,ActivityTXT_L),1,50);
        END;
      END;
      // <-- 20140606-FINA27/001-CMC02.02.00-AKH01
    END;

    PROCEDURE SetOnBehalfOf@1000000000(RecPurchLine_P@1000000000 : Record 39;VAR OnBehalfOf_P@1000000005 : Boolean;VAR ActualLocationCode_P@1000000006 : Code[10];VAR SharedSilo_P@1000000007 : Boolean);
    VAR
      RecPostedReceipt_L@1000000002 : Record 8052995;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Teste si la ligne concerne une reception "pour le compte de"
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Entr'es:
      //   - RecPurchLine_P : la ligne de la facture d'achat
      //   - OnBehalfOf_P : La variable ItemJnlLine Pour le compte de . positionner
      //   - ActualLocationCode_P : La variable ItemJnlLine Code magasin r'el . positionner
      //   - SharedSilo_P : La variable Silo partag' . positionner
      // Sorties:
      //   - Neant
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      RecPostedReceipt_L.SETRANGE("No.",RecPurchLine_P."Collection Recept No.");
      IF RecPostedReceipt_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
        IF RecPostedReceipt_L."IC Direction" = RecPostedReceipt_L."IC Direction"::Incoming THEN BEGIN
          OnBehalfOf_P := RecPostedReceipt_L."On Behalf Of" ;
          SharedSilo_P := RecPostedReceipt_L."Shared Silo"  ;
          ActualLocationCode_P := RecPostedReceipt_L."Actual Location Code";
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE UpdateReferencePrice@60000(SalesContractNo_P@60000 : Code[20]);
    VAR
      CMCSalesContract_L@60001 : Record 8053012;
      CMCBrokerageInvSchedLines_L@60002 : Record 60031;
    BEGIN
      // --> 20141219-CER77-TER01.01.00-RMZ01
      // This function will force the update of the reference price calculated only for some options
      // Input
      //    SalesContractNo_P
      // Output

      IF SalesContractNo_P = '' THEN
        EXIT;

      CLEAR(CMCSalesContract_L);
      CMCSalesContract_L.GET(SalesContractNo_P);
      IF CMCSalesContract_L.Status <> CMCSalesContract_L.Status::Closed THEN
        EXIT;

      CMCSalesContract_L.FillReferencePriceDetTablePlus;
    END;

    PROCEDURE SetDelivOnBehalfOf@1000000001(RecPurchHeader_P@1000000000 : Record 38;VAR OnBehalfOf_P@1000000005 : Boolean;VAR ActualLocationCode_P@1000000006 : Code[10];VAR SharedSilo_P@1000000007 : Boolean);
    VAR
      RecPostedDelivery_L@1000000002 : Record 8053014;
    BEGIN
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Teste si la ligne concerne un bon de sortie "pour le compte de"
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      // Entr'es:
      //   - RecPurchHeader_P : l'ent^te de la facture d'achat
      //   - OnBehalfOf_P : La variable ItemJnlLine Pour le compte de . positionner
      //   - ActualLocationCode_P : La variable ItemJnlLine Code magasin r'el . positionner
      //   - SharedSilo_P : La variable Silo partag' . positionner
      // Sorties:
      //   - Neant
      //----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
      RecPostedDelivery_L.SETRANGE("No.",RecPurchHeader_P."Source Document No.");
      IF RecPostedDelivery_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
        IF RecPostedDelivery_L."IC Direction" = RecPostedDelivery_L."IC Direction"::Incoming THEN BEGIN
          OnBehalfOf_P := RecPostedDelivery_L."On Behalf Of" ;
          IF NOT RecPostedDelivery_L."Shared Silo" AND (RecPostedDelivery_L."Actual Location Code" <> '') THEN
            OnBehalfOf_P := TRUE;
          SharedSilo_P := RecPostedDelivery_L."Shared Silo"  ;
          ActualLocationCode_P := RecPostedDelivery_L."Actual Location Code";
        END;
      END;
    END;

    PROCEDURE CreateJnlLineCostPlus@1000000007();
    VAR
      PurchDocCostPlus_L@1000000000 : Record 60121;
      CostPlusList_L@1000000001 : Record 60118;
    BEGIN
      // --> 20190109-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3446-CJY01


      CLEAR(PurchDocCostPlus_L);
      PurchDocCostPlus_L.SETRANGE("Document Type", PurchLine."Document Type");
      PurchDocCostPlus_L.SETRANGE("Document No.", PurchLine."Document No.");
      PurchDocCostPlus_L.SETRANGE("Document Line No.", PurchLine."Line No.");

      IF PurchDocCostPlus_L.FINDFIRST THEN
        REPEAT
            // --> 20200130-WI00-ADV01.01.00-3446-CJY01
            {BEFORE
            CostPlusList_L.GET(PurchDocCostPlus_L."Cost Plus Code");
            AFTER}
            IF CostPlusList_L.GET(PurchDocCostPlus_L."Cost Plus Code") THEN BEGIN
